\displaystyle\int_{{\Phi}_{t}^{-1}({\mathcal{A}})\times\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left\{\big(1-e^{\xi_{t}(x_{t},x_{0})}\big)e^{W_{t}(x_{t})}+\big(1-e^{W_{t}(x_{t})}\big)\right\}p_{X_{t},X_{0}}(x_{t},x_{0}){\textnormal{d}}x_{0}{\textnormal{d}}x_{t}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{(c)}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\int_{x_{t}\in{\Phi}_{t}^{-1}({\mathcal{A}})}\left\{1-e^{W_{t}(x_{t})}\right\}p_{X_{t}}(x_{t}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}\leq-\int_{x_{t}\in{\Phi}_{t}^{-1}({\mathcal{A}})}W_{t}(x_{t})p_{X_{t}}(x_{t}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}.

(58)

Here, (a) arises from (
53
) and uses bijection of
{\Phi}_{t}
over
{\mathbb{R}}^{d}
(so that
x_{t}
is well-defined given
x_{t-1}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d}
), (b) results from the bijection of
{\Phi}_{t}
over
{\mathbb{R}}^{d}
as well as Lemma
7
,
while the last inequality comes from the elementary inequality
1-e^{x}\leq-x
. Also, to explain why (c) is valid,
it suffices to see that for any
x_{t}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
,

\displaystyle\int_{x_{0}}\left(1-e^{\xi_{t}(x_{t},x_{0})}\right)e^{W_{t}(x_{t})}

\displaystyle p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x_{t}){\textnormal{d}}x_{0}

(59)

\displaystyle\leq-e^{W_{t}(x_{t})}\int_{x_{0}}\xi_{t}(x_{t},x_{0})p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x_{t}){\textnormal{d}}x_{0}=0,

where the inequality holds since
1-e^{x}\leq-x
for all
x\in\mathbb{R}
, and the last relation is due to (
57
).

Step 5: bounding the last term in (
58
).

Inequality (
58
) makes apparent the need to control
W_{t}(x_{t})
. The following lemma provided an upper bound on
W_{t}(x_{t})
, whose proof can be found in Appendix
C.4
.

Lemma 8

For any
x_{t}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d}
, the quantity
W_{t}(x_{t})
defined in (
55
) obeys

\displaystyle-W_{t}(x_{t})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\overline{\alpha}_{t}^{2}\eta_{t}^{2}}{(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{3}}\left\|{\mathrm{Cov}_{0|t}(x_{t})}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\frac{4(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})\eta_{t}^{2}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\left\|{\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\frac{\eta_{t}^{2}}{2(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\left\|{\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{t}\eta_{t}^{2}}{2(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathrm{Cov}_{0|t}(x_{t})\right)+\sqrt{\frac{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\eta_{t}\Delta\big(\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t}),\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})\big),

(60)

where we define

\displaystyle\Delta\big(\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t}),\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})\big)\coloneqq s_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})^{\top}\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})-\mathrm{tr}\left(\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})\right).

(61)

In order to take advantage of (
60
), it is also crucial to bound the quantity
\Delta\big(\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t}),\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})\big)
, which we study in the following lemma. The proof is provided in Appendix
C.5
.

Lemma 9

For any set
{\mathcal{A}}\subseteq{\mathbb{R}}^{d}
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{x_{t}\in{\mathcal{A}}}\Delta\big(\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t}),\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})\big)p_{X_{t}}(x_{t}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}\leq\frac{2}{\sqrt{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\varepsilon_{\mathsf{score},t}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Jacobi},1,t}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Jacobi},2,t}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Hess},t}.

With the preceding two lemmas in place, we can establish an upper bound on the integral in (
58
), as stated in the lemma below. The proof is deferred to Appendix
C.6
.

Lemma 10

The quantity
W_{t}(x_{t})
defined in (
55
) obeys

\displaystyle\int_{\Phi_{t}^{-1}({\mathcal{A}})}-W_{t}(x_{t})p_{X_{t}}(x_{t}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\widetilde{\sigma}_{t}^{2}\eta_{t}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}{\mathbb{E}}_{X_{t}}\big[\mathrm{tr}\big(\mathrm{Cov}_{0|t}(X_{t})\big)\big]+\frac{4(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{t}^{4}{\mathbb{E}}_{X_{t}}\big[\left\|{\mathrm{Cov}_{0|t}(X_{t})}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\big]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})\eta_{t}^{2}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Jacobi},1,t}^{2}+\frac{\eta_{t}^{2}}{2(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\varepsilon_{\mathsf{score},t}^{2}

(62)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2\eta_{t}\sqrt{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\bigg\{\frac{\varepsilon_{\mathsf{score},t}}{\sqrt{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Jacobi},1,t}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Jacobi},2,t}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Hess},t}\bigg\}.

Step 6: bounding
\mathsf{TV}(p_{X_{t-1}},p_{\Phi_{t}(X_{t})})
.

Putting Lemma
10
and (
58
) together with the definition of the total variation yields

\displaystyle\mathsf{TV}(p_{X_{t-1}},p_{\Phi_{t}(X_{t})})=\sup\limits_{{\mathcal{A}}\subseteq\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left\{{\mathbb{P}}_{X_{t-1}}({\mathcal{A}})-{\mathbb{P}}_{\Phi_{t}(X_{t})}({\mathcal{A}})\right\}\leq\frac{\widetilde{\sigma}_{t}^{2}\eta_{t}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}{\mathbb{E}}_{X_{t}}\big[\mathrm{tr}\big(\mathrm{Cov}_{0|t}(X_{t})\big)\big]

\displaystyle+\frac{4(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{t}^{4}{\mathbb{E}}_{X_{t}}\left[\left\|{\mathrm{Cov}_{0|t}(X_{t})}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right]{+}\frac{4(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})\eta_{t}^{2}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Jacobi},1,t}^{2}{+}\frac{\eta_{t}^{2}}{2(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\varepsilon_{\mathsf{score},t}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{2\eta_{t}\sqrt{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\bigg\{\frac{\varepsilon_{\mathsf{score},t}}{\sqrt{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Jacobi},1,t}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Jacobi},2,t}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Hess},t}\bigg\}.

(63)

In addition, Lemma
3
tells us that

\displaystyle\frac{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{\widetilde{\sigma}_{t}^{4}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|{\mathrm{Cov}_{0|t}(X_{t})}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{3(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{\widetilde{\sigma}_{t}^{2}}\biggl\{{\mathbb{E}}\big[\mathrm{tr}\big(\mathrm{Cov}_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(X_{t})\big)\big]

\displaystyle\qquad-{\mathbb{E}}\big[\mathrm{tr}\big(\mathrm{Cov}_{X_{0}|X_{t-1}}(X_{t-1})\big)\big]\biggr\}+\frac{1}{T^{10}}.

(64)

Taking (
49
), (
63
) and (
64
) collectively, we can demonstrate that

\displaystyle\mathsf{TV}(p_{X_{t-1}},p_{Y_{t-1}})\leq\mathsf{TV}(p_{X_{t-1}},p_{\Phi_{t}(X_{t})})+\mathsf{TV}(p_{X_{t}},p_{Y_{t}})\leq\mathsf{TV}(p_{X_{t}},p_{Y_{t}})

\displaystyle{+}\underbrace{\frac{\widetilde{\sigma}_{t}^{2}(\eta_{t}+3\alpha_{t}-3\overline{\alpha}_{t})}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathrm{Cov}_{0|t}(X_{t})\right)\right]{-}\frac{3(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathrm{Cov}_{0|t-1}(X_{t-1})\right)\right]+\frac{1}{T^{10}}}_{\eqqcolon\,{\mathcal{S}}_{t,1}}

(65)

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\frac{2\eta_{t}\sqrt{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\bigg\{\frac{\varepsilon_{\mathsf{score},t}}{\sqrt{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Jacobi},1,t}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Jacobi},2,t}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Hess},t}\bigg\}{+}\frac{4(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})\eta_{t}^{2}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Jacobi},1,t}^{2}+\frac{\eta_{t}^{2}}{2(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\varepsilon_{\mathsf{score},t}^{2}}_{\eqqcolon\,{\mathcal{S}}_{t,2}}.

Here, we divide the residual terms into two parts,
{\mathcal{S}}_{t,1}
and
{\mathcal{S}}_{t,2}
: the term
{\mathcal{S}}_{t,1}
reflects the discretization error, while
{\mathcal{S}}_{t,2}
is associated with the score estimation error.
The following lemma helps control the two sums in (
65
), with the proof postponed to Appendix
C.7
.

Lemma 11

There exist some universal constants
C_{10},C_{11}>0
such that

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{{t}={2}}^{T}{\mathcal{S}}_{t,1}+\sum\limits_{{t}={2}}^{T}{\mathcal{S}}_{t,2}\leq C_{10}\frac{k\log^{3}T}{T}+C_{11}\left(\varepsilon_{\mathsf{score}}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Jacobi},1}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Jacobi},2}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Hess}}\right)\log T.

(66)

Step 7: putting all pieces together.

To finish up, applying inequality (
65
) recursively from
1
to
T
, and combining (
83
) and (
84
), we reach

\displaystyle\mathsf{TV}(p_{X_{1}},p_{Y_{1}})

\displaystyle\leq\mathsf{TV}(p_{X_{T}},p_{Y_{T}})+\sum\limits_{{t}={2}}^{T}{\mathcal{S}}_{t,1}+\sum\limits_{{t}={2}}^{T}{\mathcal{S}}_{t,2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{10}\frac{k\log^{3}T}{T}+C_{11}\left(\varepsilon_{\mathsf{score}}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Jacobi},1}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Jacobi},2}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Hess}}\right)\log T+\mathsf{TV}(p_{X_{T}},p_{Y_{T}})

\displaystyle\leq C_{10}\frac{k\log^{3}T}{T}+C_{11}\left(\varepsilon_{\mathsf{score}}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Jacobi},1}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Jacobi},2}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Hess}}\right)\log T+\frac{1}{T^{10}},

where the last inequality arises from
Li and Yan (2024a, Lemma 10)
. This concludes the proof of Theorem
2
.

C.2
Proof of Lemma
6

To prove this lemma, we make use of the following global inverse function theorem, which is presented in
(
Ruzhansky and Sugimoto, 2015
)

Lemma 12
(Theorem 2.2 in
(
Ruzhansky and Sugimoto, 2015
)
)

A
C^{1}
-map
f:\mathbb{R}^{d}\to\mathbb{R}^{d}
is a
C^{1}
-diffeomorphism iff the Jacobian
\det\left(\nabla f(x)\right)
never vanishes and
\left\|{f(x)}\right\|_{2}\to\infty
whenever
\left\|{x}\right\|_{2}\to\infty
.

For any
x\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d}
and any nonzero vector
v\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d}
, Tweedie’s formula (
46
) tells us that the mapping
{\Phi}_{t}
(cf. (
48
)) obeys

\displaystyle v^{\top}\frac{\partial{\Phi}_{t}(x)}{\partial x}v

\displaystyle=v^{\top}\bigg(I+\eta_{t}\frac{\partial s_{t}(x)}{\partial x}\bigg)v

\displaystyle=\left\|{v}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\eta_{t}v^{\top}\frac{\partial s_{t}(x)}{\partial x}v\geq\left\|{v}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\frac{1}{4}\left\|{v}\right\|_{2}^{2}>0.

Here, the penultimate relation follows from Assumption
3
.
This result implies that
\frac{\partial{\Phi}_{t}(x)}{\partial x}
never vanishes at all points
x\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d}
uniformly.
On the other hand, for any
x\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\left\|{\Phi_{t}(x)}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|{x+\eta_{t}s_{t}(x)}\right\|_{2}^{2}=\left\|{x}\right\|_{2}^{2}+2\eta_{t}x^{\top}s_{t}(x)+\eta_{t}^{2}\left\|{s_{t}(x)}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\left\|{x}\right\|_{2}^{2}+2\eta_{t}x^{\top}s_{t}(x)\overset{(a)}{=}\left\|{x}\right\|_{2}^{2}+2\eta_{t}x^{\top}\frac{\partial s_{t}(\vartheta)}{\partial\vartheta}x

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\geq}\left\|{x}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\left\|{x}\right\|_{2}^{2}=\frac{1}{2}\left\|{x}\right\|_{2}^{2}.

Here
(a)
holds by making use of the Lagrange mean value theorem,
(b)
follows from Assumption
3
. Based on this result, we know that
\lim\limits_{x\to\infty}\left\|{\Phi_{t}(x)}\right\|_{2}=\infty
. This completes the proof of the lemma.

C.3
Proof of Lemma
7

In what follows, we would like to establish the following identities:

\displaystyle{\mathcal{T}}_{1}(x_{t},x_{0})

\displaystyle=\det\left(I+\sqrt{\frac{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{t}\eta_{t}}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}}\mathrm{Cov}_{0|t}(x_{t})+\sqrt{\frac{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\eta_{t}\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})\right),

(67a)

\displaystyle\log{\mathcal{T}}_{2}(x_{t},x_{0})

\displaystyle=\xi_{t}(x_{t},x_{0})-\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{t}\eta_{t}}{(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\left(1-\frac{\eta_{t}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathrm{Cov}_{0|t}(x_{t})\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad-\sqrt{\frac{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\eta_{t}s_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})^{\top}\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})-\frac{\eta_{t}^{2}}{2(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\left\|{\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})}\right\|_{2}^{2}.

(67b)

The advertised relation (
56
) then follows immediately from (
67
). The remainder of this proof thus comes down to establishing (
67
).

C.3.1
Controlling the term
{\mathcal{T}}_{1}(x_{t},x_{0})

Let us first look at the term
{\mathcal{T}}_{1}(x_{t},x_{0})
, which satisfies

\displaystyle{\mathcal{T}}_{1}(x_{t},x_{0})

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}\right)^{\frac{d}{2}}\det\left(\frac{\partial\big(x_{t}+\eta_{t}s_{t}(x_{t})\big)/\sqrt{{\alpha}_{t}}}{\partial x_{t}}\right)=\left(\frac{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\right)^{\frac{d}{2}}\det\left(\frac{\partial\big(x_{t}+\eta_{t}s_{t}(x_{t})\big)}{\partial x_{t}}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\right)^{\frac{d}{2}}\det\left(I+\eta_{t}\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{t}}s_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})+\eta_{t}\left(\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{t}}s_{t}(x_{t})-\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{t}}s_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\right)\right)

\displaystyle\overset{\text{(a)}}{=}\left(\frac{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\right)^{\frac{d}{2}}\det\left(I+\eta_{t}\left\{\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}}\mathrm{Cov}_{0|t}(x_{t})-\frac{1}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}I\right\}+\eta_{t}\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})\right)

\displaystyle=\det\left(\sqrt{\frac{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\left(1-\frac{\eta_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\right)I+\sqrt{\frac{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{t}\eta_{t}}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}}\mathrm{Cov}_{0|t}(x_{t})+\sqrt{\frac{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\eta_{t}\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})\right)

\displaystyle\overset{\text{(b)}}{=}\det\left(I+\sqrt{\frac{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{t}\eta_{t}}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}}\mathrm{Cov}_{0|t}(x_{t})+\sqrt{\frac{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\eta_{t}\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})\right)

(68)

as claimed. Here, (a) arises from Tweedie’s formula (
46
),
whereas (b) follows since (see (
48
))

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\left(1-\frac{\eta_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\right)=\sqrt{\frac{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\frac{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})-\eta_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}=\sqrt{\frac{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\frac{\sqrt{(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}=1.

(69)

C.3.2
Controlling the term
{\mathcal{T}}_{2}(x_{t},x_{0})

Next, we turn attention to the term
{\mathcal{T}}_{2}(x_{t},x_{0})
, which obeys

\displaystyle\log{\mathcal{T}}_{2}(x_{t},x_{0})

\displaystyle=\frac{\left\|{x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}}\right\|_{2}^{2}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}-\frac{\left\|{\big(x_{t}+\eta_{t}s_{t}(x_{t})\big)/\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}x_{0}}\right\|_{2}^{2}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1})}

(70)

\displaystyle=\frac{\left\|{x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}}\right\|_{2}^{2}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}-\frac{\left\|{x_{t}+\eta_{t}s_{t}(x_{t})-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}}\right\|_{2}^{2}}{2(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}.

Regarding the term
x_{t}+\eta_{t}s_{t}(x_{t})-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}
, we can apply Tweedie’s formula (
46
) to show that

\displaystyle x_{t}+\eta_{t}s_{t}(x_{t})-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}=\left(1-\frac{\eta_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\right)x_{t}+\frac{\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\eta_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0|t}(x_{t})-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}+\eta_{t}\big(s_{t}(x_{t})-s_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\big)

\displaystyle=\left(1-\frac{\eta_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\right)(x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0})+\frac{\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\eta_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0|t}(x_{t})-\frac{\eta_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}+\eta_{t}\big(s_{t}(x_{t})-s_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\big)

\displaystyle=\left(1-\frac{\eta_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\right)(x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0})+\frac{\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\eta_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\big(\mu_{0|t}(x_{t})-x_{0}\big)+\eta_{t}\big(s_{t}(x_{t})-s_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\big)

\displaystyle=\left(1-\frac{\eta_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\right)(x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0})+\frac{\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\eta_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\big(\mu_{0|t}(x_{t})-x_{0}\big)+\eta_{t}\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t}).

Substitution into (
70
) yields

\displaystyle\log{\mathcal{T}}_{2}(x_{t},x_{0})=\frac{\left\|{x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}}\right\|_{2}^{2}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}-\frac{1}{2(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\left(1-\frac{\eta_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\right)^{2}\left\|{x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}}\right\|_{2}^{2}

(71)

\displaystyle+\left(1-\frac{\eta_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\right)\frac{\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\eta_{t}}{(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}(x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0})^{\top}\big(x_{0}-\mu_{0|t}(x_{t})\big)-\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{t}\eta_{t}^{2}}{2(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}}\left\|{x_{0}-\mu_{0|t}(x_{t})}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{\eta_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\left(1{-}\frac{\eta_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\right)\big(x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0|t}(x_{t})\big)^{\top}\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})

\displaystyle+\frac{\eta_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\big(\mu_{0|t}(x_{t}){-}x_{0}\big)^{\top}\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})+\frac{\eta_{t}^{2}}{2(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\left\|{\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})}\right\|_{2}^{2}.

In the sequel, we control each term of the above display separately.

•

Firstly, it follows from (
69
) that

\frac{1}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}-\frac{1}{2(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\left(1-\frac{\eta_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\right)^{2}=\frac{1}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}-\frac{1}{2(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\frac{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}=0,

thus implying that

\frac{\left\|{x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}}\right\|_{2}^{2}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}-\frac{1}{2(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\left(1-\frac{\eta_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\right)^{2}\left\|{x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}}\right\|_{2}^{2}=0.

•

Secondly, invoking Tweedie’s formula (
46
) once again yields

\displaystyle\frac{\eta_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\left(1{-}\frac{\eta_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\right)\big(x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0|t}(x_{t})\big)^{\top}\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})=-\frac{\eta_{t}(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}-\eta_{t})}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}s_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})^{\top}\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t}).

•

Thirdly, consider the following component of
\log{\mathcal{T}}_{2}(x_{t},x_{0})
:

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{23}(x_{t},x_{0})

\displaystyle\coloneqq\left(1-\frac{\eta_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\right)\frac{\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\eta_{t}}{(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}(x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0})^{\top}\big(x_{0}-\mu_{0|t}(x_{t})\big)

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{t}\eta_{t}^{2}}{2(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}}\left\|{x_{0}-\mu_{0\,|\,t}(x_{t})}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{\eta_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}(\mu_{0|t}(x_{t})-x_{0})^{\top}\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t}).

(72)

Taking the expectation of the above term (
72
) under the conditional distribution
p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}
, we find that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{X_{0}\sim p_{X_{0}|X_{t}=x_{t}}}\big[\mathcal{T}_{23}(x_{t},X_{0})\big]

\displaystyle=-\left(1-\frac{\eta_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\right)\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{t}\eta_{t}}{(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\mathrm{tr}\big(\mathrm{Cov}_{0\,|\,t}(x_{t})\big)

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{t}\eta_{t}^{2}}{2(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}}\mathrm{tr}\big(\mathrm{Cov}_{0\,|\,t}(x_{t})\big)

\displaystyle=-\left(1-\frac{\eta_{t}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\right)\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{t}\eta_{t}}{(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\mathrm{tr}\big(\mathrm{Cov}_{0\,|\,t}(x_{t})\big).

Therefore, the quantity
\xi_{t}(x_{t},x_{0})
defined in (
54
) obeys

\displaystyle\xi_{t}(x_{t},x_{0})

\displaystyle=\mathcal{T}_{23}(x_{t},x_{0})-\mathbb{E}_{X_{0}\sim p_{X_{0}|X_{t}=x_{t}}}\big[\mathcal{T}_{23}(x_{t},X_{0})\big],

it can be easily verified that equation (
57
) holds.

Thus, substituting the preceding relations back into Eqn. (
71
), we can establish (
67b
) as follows:

\displaystyle\log{\mathcal{T}}_{2}(x_{t},x_{0})

\displaystyle=\xi_{t}(x_{t},x_{0})-\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{t}\eta_{t}}{(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\left(1-\frac{\eta_{t}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathrm{Cov}_{0|t}(x_{t})\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad-\frac{\eta_{t}(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}-\eta_{t})}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}s_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})^{\top}\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})-\frac{\eta_{t}^{2}}{2(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\left\|{\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\xi_{t}(x_{t},x_{0})-\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{t}\eta_{t}}{(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\left(1-\frac{\eta_{t}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathrm{Cov}_{0|t}(x_{t})\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad-\sqrt{\frac{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\eta_{t}s_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})^{\top}\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})-\frac{\eta_{t}^{2}}{2(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\left\|{\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})}\right\|_{2}^{2},

(73)

where the last equality holds since

\frac{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}-\eta_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}=\frac{\sqrt{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}=\sqrt{\frac{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}.

C.4
Proof of Lemma
8

For any
x_{t}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d}
,
applying Lemma
5
reveals that

\displaystyle-\log\det

\displaystyle\left(I+\sqrt{\frac{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{t}\eta_{t}}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}}\mathrm{Cov}_{0|t}(x_{t})+\sqrt{\frac{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\eta_{t}\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\overline{\alpha}_{t}^{2}\eta_{t}^{2}}{(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{3}}\left\|{\mathrm{Cov}_{0|t}(x_{t})}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\frac{4(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})\eta_{t}^{2}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\left\|{\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\sqrt{\frac{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{t}\eta_{t}}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathrm{Cov}_{0|t}(x_{t})\right)-\sqrt{\frac{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\eta_{t}\mathrm{tr}\big(\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})\big),

provided that
\sqrt{\frac{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\eta_{t}\left\|{\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})}\right\|_{2}\leq\frac{1}{4}
.
Combining this with the definition (
55
) of
W_{t}
results in

\displaystyle-W_{t}(x_{t})

\displaystyle=\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{t}\eta_{t}}{(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\left(1-\frac{\eta_{t}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\right)\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathrm{Cov}_{0|t}(x_{t})\right)+\frac{\eta_{t}^{2}}{2(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\left\|{\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle+\sqrt{\frac{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\eta_{t}s_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})^{\top}\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})-\log\det\left(I+\sqrt{\frac{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{t}\eta_{t}}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}}\mathrm{Cov}_{0|t}(x_{t}){+}\sqrt{\frac{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\eta_{t}\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\overline{\alpha}_{t}^{2}\eta_{t}^{2}}{(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{3}}\left\|{\mathrm{Cov}_{0|t}(x_{t})}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\frac{4(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})\eta_{t}^{2}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\left\|{\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\frac{\eta_{t}^{2}}{2(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\left\|{\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\left[\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{t}\eta_{t}}{(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\left(1-\frac{\eta_{t}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\right)-\sqrt{\frac{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{t}\eta_{t}}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}}\right]\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathrm{Cov}_{0|t}(x_{t})\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\sqrt{\frac{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\eta_{t}\Big(s_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})^{\top}\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})-\mathrm{tr}\left(\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})\right)\Big).

(74)

Recalling our choice of the coefficient
\eta_{t}=(1-\alpha_{t})\big/\big(1+\sqrt{\frac{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\big)
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{t}\eta_{t}}{(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\left(1-\frac{\eta_{t}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\right)-\sqrt{\frac{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{t}\eta_{t}}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad=\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{t}\eta_{t}}{(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\left(1-\frac{\eta_{t}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}-\sqrt{\frac{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad=\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{t}\eta_{t}}{(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\left(1-\frac{\eta_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}-\sqrt{\frac{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}+\frac{\eta_{t}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad=\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{t}\eta_{t}^{2}}{2(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}},

where the penultimate equality holds due to Eqn. (
69
). Substituting this result into (
74
) yields

\displaystyle-W_{t}(x_{t})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\overline{\alpha}_{t}^{2}\eta_{t}^{2}}{(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{3}}\left\|{\mathrm{Cov}_{0|t}(x_{t})}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\frac{4(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})\eta_{t}^{2}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\left\|{\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\frac{\eta_{t}^{2}}{2(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\left\|{\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})}\right\|_{2}^{2}

(75)

\displaystyle+\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{t}\eta_{t}^{2}}{2(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathrm{Cov}_{0|t}(x_{t})\right)+\sqrt{\frac{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\eta_{t}\underbrace{\left(s_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})^{\top}\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})-\mathrm{tr}\left(\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})\right)\right)}_{\eqqcolon\Delta(\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t}),\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t}))}.

C.5
Proof of Lemma
9

To begin with, consider the inner product term
s_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})^{\top}\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})
. For any set
{\mathcal{A}}
, one can derive

\displaystyle\int_{x_{t}\in{\mathcal{A}}}s_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})^{\top}\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})

\displaystyle p_{X_{t}}(x_{t}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}=\int_{x_{t}\in{\mathcal{A}}}\frac{1}{{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\big(x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0|t}(x_{t})\big)^{\top}\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})p_{X_{t}}(x_{t}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathcal{A}}\times{\mathcal{X}}_{\mathsf{data}}}\frac{1}{{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}(x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0})^{\top}\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x_{t}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}{\textnormal{d}}x_{0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathcal{A}}\times{\mathcal{X}}_{\mathsf{data}}}\frac{1}{{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}(x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0})^{\top}\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0})p_{X_{0}}(x_{0}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}{\textnormal{d}}x_{0}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}\times{\mathcal{X}}_{\mathsf{data}}}\left|{\frac{1}{{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}(x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0})^{\top}\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})}\right|p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0})p_{X_{0}}(x_{0}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}{\textnormal{d}}x_{0},

(76)

where the first identity follows from Tweedie’s formula (
46
).
For any given point
x_{0}\in{\mathcal{X}}_{\mathsf{data}}
, applying the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality gives

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}

\displaystyle\left|{\frac{1}{{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}(x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0})^{\top}\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})}\right|p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}

(77)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left(\frac{1}{{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}(x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0})^{\top}\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})\right)^{2}p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\frac{1}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}}\Big\langle\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})^{\top},\,(x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0})(x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0})^{\top}\Big\rangle p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Note that for given
x_{0}
, we know that
X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}=x_{0}
has distribution
{\mathcal{N}}\big(\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0},(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{-1}I\big)
. As a result,

\displaystyle\nabla^{2}_{x_{t}}p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0})

\displaystyle=\nabla^{2}_{x_{t}}\left\{\left(\frac{1}{2\pi(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\right)^{\frac{d}{2}}\exp\left(-\frac{\left\|{x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}}\right\|_{2}^{2}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{2\pi(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\right)^{\frac{d}{2}}\nabla_{x_{t}}\left\{-\frac{x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\exp\left(-\frac{\left\|{x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}}\right\|_{2}^{2}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{2\pi(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\right)^{\frac{d}{2}}e^{-\frac{\|x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\|_{2}^{2}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}}\left\{\frac{(x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0})(x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0})^{\top}}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}}-\frac{1}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}I\right\}

\displaystyle=p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0})\left\{\frac{(x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0})(x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0})^{\top}}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}}-\frac{1}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}I\right\},

which in turn gives

\frac{(x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0})(x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0})^{\top}}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}}p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0})=\nabla^{2}_{x_{t}}p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0})+p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0})\frac{1}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}I.

Substituting this into (
77
) yields

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left|{\frac{1}{{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}(x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0})^{\top}\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})}\right|p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left\langle\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})^{\top},\nabla^{2}_{x_{t}}p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0})+\frac{1}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0})I\right\rangle{\textnormal{d}}x_{t}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left\langle\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})^{\top},\nabla^{2}_{x_{t}}p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0})\right\rangle{\textnormal{d}}x_{t}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left\|{\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})}\right\|_{2}^{2}p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}},

(78)

where the last inequality follows since
\sqrt{a+b}\leq\sqrt{a}+\sqrt{b}
for all
a,b\geq 0
. This leaves us with two terms to control.

With regards to the first term of the above bound (
78
), we make the observation that

\left\langle\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})^{\top},\nabla^{2}p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0})\right\rangle=\sum\limits_{{i}={1}}^{d}\sum\limits_{{j}={1}}^{d}[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{i}[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{j}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial x_{i}\partial x_{j}}p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0}).

Here and throughout,
[v]_{i}
represents the
i
-coordinate of the vector
v
.
We can start by analyzing each
(i,j)
component. In fact, for any
1\leq i,j\leq d
, it holds that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{i}[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{j}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial x_{i}\partial x_{j}}p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}\overset{\text{(a)}}{=}

\displaystyle{-}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{i}}\big([\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{i}[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{j}\big)\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{j}}p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{(b)}}{=}

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial x_{i}\partial x_{j}}\big([\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{i}[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{j}\big)p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t},

(79)

where (a) and (b) apply the integration by parts formula with respect to
x_{i}
and
x_{j}
, respectively. Denoting by
[A]_{ij}
the
(i,j)
-th element of the matrix
A
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial x_{i}\partial x_{j}}\big([\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{i}[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{j}\big)

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial x_{i}\partial x_{j}}[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{i}\right)[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{j}+[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{i}\left(\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial x_{i}\partial x_{j}}[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{j}\right)

\displaystyle+[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{ij}[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{ji}+[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{ii}[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{jj},

where we recall the definition of
\varepsilon_{t}^{\mathsf{J}}
in (
43
).
Substitution into (
79
) yields

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}

\displaystyle\left\langle\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})^{\top},\nabla^{2}p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0})\right\rangle{\textnormal{d}}x_{t}=\sum\limits_{{i}={1}}^{d}\sum\limits_{{j}={1}}^{d}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{i}[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{j}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial x_{i}\partial x_{j}}p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}

(80)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{{i}={1}}^{d}\sum\limits_{{j}={1}}^{d}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial x_{i}\partial x_{j}}\left([\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{i}[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{j}\right)p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{{i}={1}}^{d}\sum\limits_{{j}={1}}^{d}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left\{\left(\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial x_{i}\partial x_{j}}[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{i}\right)[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{j}+[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{i}\left(\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial x_{i}\partial x_{j}}[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{j}\right)\right\}p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{{i}={1}}^{d}\sum\limits_{{j}={1}}^{d}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left\{[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{ij}[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{ji}+[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{ii}[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{jj}\right\}p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}.

We now proceed to investigate each term in the above expression.

•

For any two indices
i
and
j
, we have

\left(\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial x_{i}\partial x_{j}}\left[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})\right]_{i}\right)[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{j}=\left(\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{j}}\left[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})\right]_{ii}\right)[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{j},

and consequently,

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{{i}={1}}^{d}\sum\limits_{{j}={1}}^{d}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}

\displaystyle\left\{\left(\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial x_{i}\partial x_{j}}[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{i}\right)[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{j}+[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{i}\left(\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial x_{i}\partial x_{j}}[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{j}\right)\right\}p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}

\displaystyle=2\sum\limits_{{j}={1}}^{d}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{j}}\left(\sum\limits_{{i}={1}}^{d}[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{ii}\right)[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{j}p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}2\left\langle\nabla\mathrm{tr}\big(\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})\big),\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})\right\rangle p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}.

•

Next, for terms of the form
[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{ij}[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{ji}
and
[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{ii}[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{jj}
, simple calculations yield

\displaystyle\Bigg|\sum\limits_{{i}={1}}^{d}\sum\limits_{{j}={1}}^{d}[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{ij}[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{ji}\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|{\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2};

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{{i}={1}}^{d}\sum\limits_{{j}={1}}^{d}[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{ii}[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{jj}

\displaystyle=\left(\sum\limits_{{i}={1}}^{d}[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{ii}\right)\left(\sum\limits_{{j}={1}}^{d}[\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})]_{jj}\right)=\big(\mathrm{tr}(\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t}))\big)^{2}.

•

Substituting these results into (
80
), we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}

\displaystyle\left\langle\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})^{\top},\nabla^{2}p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0})\right\rangle{\textnormal{d}}x_{t}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left\{2\left\langle\nabla\mathrm{tr}\left(\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})\right),\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})\right\rangle+\big(\mathrm{tr}(\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t}))\big)^{2}+\left\|{\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right\}p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left\{\left\|{\nabla\mathrm{tr}(\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t}))}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\left\|{\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\big(\mathrm{tr}(\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t}))\big)^{2}+\left\|{\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right\}p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}.

As a result, one can further deduce that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{X}_{\mathsf{data}}}\bigg(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left\langle\varepsilon_{t}^{\mathsf{sc}}(x_{t})\varepsilon_{t}^{\mathsf{sc}}(x_{t})^{\top},\nabla^{2}p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0})\right\rangle{\textnormal{d}}x_{t}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}p_{X_{0}}(x_{0}){\textnormal{d}}x_{0}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{\mathcal{X}_{\mathsf{data}}}\bigg(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\Big\{\left\|{\nabla\mathrm{tr}(\varepsilon_{t}^{\mathsf{J}}(x_{t}))}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\left\|{\varepsilon_{t}^{\mathsf{sc}}(x_{t})}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\big(\mathrm{tr}(\varepsilon_{t}^{\mathsf{J}}(x_{t}))\big)^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\left\|{\varepsilon_{t}^{\mathsf{J}}(x_{t})}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\Big\}p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}p_{X_{0}}(x_{0}){\textnormal{d}}x_{0}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(\int_{\mathcal{X}_{\mathsf{data}}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left\{\left\|{\nabla\mathrm{tr}(\varepsilon_{t}^{\mathsf{J}}(x_{t}))}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\left\|{\varepsilon_{t}^{\mathsf{sc}}(x_{t})}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\big(\mathrm{tr}(\varepsilon_{t}^{\mathsf{J}}(x_{t}))\big)^{2}+\left\|{\varepsilon_{t}^{\mathsf{J}}(x_{t})}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right\}p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}{\textnormal{d}}x_{0}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=\bigg(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left\{\left\|{\nabla\mathrm{tr}(\varepsilon_{t}^{\mathsf{J}}(x_{t}))}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\left\|{\varepsilon_{t}^{\mathsf{sc}}(x_{t})}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\big(\mathrm{tr}(\varepsilon_{t}^{\mathsf{J}}(x_{t}))\big)^{2}+\left\|{\varepsilon_{t}^{\mathsf{J}}(x_{t})}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right\}p_{X_{t}}(x_{t}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left\|{\nabla\mathrm{tr}(\varepsilon_{t}^{\mathsf{J}}(x_{t}))}\right\|_{2}^{2}p_{X_{t}}(x_{t}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\bigg(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left\|{\varepsilon_{t}^{\mathsf{sc}}(x_{t})}\right\|_{2}^{2}p_{X_{t}}(x_{t}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigg(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\big(\mathrm{tr}(\varepsilon_{t}^{\mathsf{J}}(x_{t}))\big)^{2}p_{X_{t}}(x_{t}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\bigg(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left\|{\varepsilon_{t}^{\mathsf{J}}(x_{t})}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}p_{X_{t}}(x_{t}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}},

(81)

where the second inequality comes from Jensen’s inequality.

With the above result in place, one can readily combine it with (
76
) and (
78
) to reach

\displaystyle\int_{x_{t}\in{\mathcal{A}}}s_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})^{\top}

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})p_{X_{t}}(x_{t}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\int_{{\mathcal{X}}_{\mathsf{data}}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left\|{\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})}\right\|_{2}^{2}p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}p_{X_{0}}(x_{0}){\textnormal{d}}x_{0}

(82)

\displaystyle+\int_{x_{0}\in{\mathcal{X}}_{\mathsf{data}}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left\langle\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})^{\top},\nabla^{2}p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0})\right\rangle{\textnormal{d}}x_{t}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}p_{X_{0}}(x_{0}){\textnormal{d}}x_{0}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1+\frac{1}{\sqrt{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\right)\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left\|{\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})}\right\|_{2}^{2}p_{X_{t}}(x_{t}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left\|{\nabla\mathrm{tr}\big(\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})\big)}\right\|_{2}^{2}p_{X_{t}}(x_{t}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left\|{\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}p_{X_{t}}(x_{t}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\mathrm{tr}\big(\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})\big)^{2}p_{X_{t}}(x_{t}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\sqrt{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\varepsilon_{\mathsf{score},t}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Jacobi},1,t}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Jacobi},2,t}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Hess},t}.

Thus, we can apply Jensen’s inequality once again to arrive at

\displaystyle\int_{x_{t}\in{\mathcal{A}}}\Delta\big(\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t}),\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})\big)

\displaystyle p_{X_{t}}(x_{t}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}=\int_{x_{t}\in{\mathcal{A}}}\left\{s_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})^{\top}\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})-\mathrm{tr}\big(\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})\big)\right\}p_{X_{t}}(x_{t}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\sqrt{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\varepsilon_{\mathsf{score},t}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Jacobi},1,t}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Jacobi},2,t}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Hess},t}+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left|{\mathrm{tr}\big(\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})\big)}\right|p_{X_{t}}(x_{t}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\sqrt{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\varepsilon_{\mathsf{score},t}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Jacobi},1,t}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Jacobi},2,t}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Hess},t}.

C.6
Proof of Lemma
10

Before applying Lemma
8
to control
W_{t}(x_{t})
, let us first look at some key coefficients in the inequality (
60
) therein. As in Lemma
3
, define
\widetilde{\sigma}_{t}^{2}\coloneq\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{t}(1-\alpha_{t})}{(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}
. For the DDIM coefficient choice (
11a
), it holds that
\eta_{t}=\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{1+\sqrt{\frac{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}}\leq 1-\alpha_{t}
, and hence we can derive

\displaystyle\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{t}^{2}\eta_{t}^{2}}{(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{3}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\cdot\left(\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{t}(1-\alpha_{t})}{(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\right)^{2}=\frac{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{t}^{4};

\displaystyle\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{t}\eta_{t}^{2}}{(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\eta_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\cdot\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{t}(1-\alpha_{t})}{(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}=\frac{\widetilde{\sigma}_{t}^{2}\eta_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}.

Substitution into (
60
) allows one to control the term
\int_{x_{t}\in\Phi_{t}^{-1}({\mathcal{A}})}W_{t}(x_{t})p_{X_{t}}(x_{t}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}
as follows:

\displaystyle\int_{\Phi_{t}^{-1}({\mathcal{A}})}

\displaystyle-W_{t}(x_{t})p_{X_{t}}(x_{t}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{\Phi_{t}^{-1}({\mathcal{A}})}\left\{\frac{\widetilde{\sigma}_{t}^{2}\eta_{t}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathrm{Cov}_{0|t}(x_{t})\right)+\frac{4(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{t}^{4}\left\|{\mathrm{Cov}_{0|t}(x_{t})}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right\}p_{X_{t}}(x_{t}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{\Phi_{t}^{-1}({\mathcal{A}})}\left\{\frac{4(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})\eta_{t}^{2}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\left\|{\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\frac{\eta_{t}^{2}}{2(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\left\|{\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right\}p_{X_{t}}(x_{t}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sqrt{\frac{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\eta_{t}\int_{\Phi_{t}^{-1}({\mathcal{A}})}\Delta\big(\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t}),\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})\big)p_{X_{t}}(x_{t}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left\{\frac{\widetilde{\sigma}_{t}^{2}\eta_{t}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathrm{Cov}_{0|t}(x_{t})\right)+\frac{4(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{t}^{4}\left\|{\mathrm{Cov}_{0|t}(x_{t})}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right\}p_{X_{t}}(x_{t}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left\{\frac{4(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})\eta_{t}^{2}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\left\|{\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\frac{\eta_{t}^{2}}{2(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\left\|{\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right\}p_{X_{t}}(x_{t}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sqrt{\frac{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\eta_{t}\sup\limits_{{\mathcal{A}}\subseteq\mathbb{R}^{d}}\int_{{\mathcal{A}}}\Delta\big(\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t}),\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})\big)p_{X_{t}}(x_{t}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}.

Additionally, in view of Lemma
9
, we know that for any measurable set
{\mathcal{A}}\subseteq\mathbb{R}^{d}
,

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\eta_{t}\int_{{\mathcal{A}}}

\displaystyle\Delta\big(\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t}),\varepsilon^{\mathsf{J}}_{t}(x_{t})\big)p_{X_{t}}(x_{t}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\eta_{t}\sqrt{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\left\{\frac{\varepsilon_{\mathsf{score},t}}{\sqrt{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Jacobi},1,t}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Jacobi},2,t}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Hess},t}\right\}.

Taking the above pieces together, we can readily conclude the proof of the advertised result.

C.7
Proof of Lemma
11

Let us first cope with
\sum\limits_{{t}={1}}^{T}{\mathcal{S}}_{t,1}
, which concerns the accumulated discretization error. A little algebra gives

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{{t}={1}}^{T}{\mathcal{S}}_{t,1}

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{{t}={1}}^{T-1}\left(\frac{\widetilde{\sigma}_{t}^{2}\eta_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}+\frac{3(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})\widetilde{\sigma}_{t}^{2}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}-\frac{3(\alpha_{t+1}-\overline{\alpha}_{t+1})\widetilde{\sigma}_{t+1}^{2}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t+1}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathrm{Cov}_{0|t}(X_{t})\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\widetilde{\sigma}_{T}^{2}(\eta_{T}+\alpha_{T}-\overline{\alpha}_{T})}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{T}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathrm{Cov}_{0|T}(X_{T})\right)\right]+\frac{1}{T^{9}}.

Applying Lemma
4
and the basic property (
45
),
we can show that

\displaystyle\frac{\widetilde{\sigma}_{t}^{2}\eta_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{+}\frac{3(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})\widetilde{\sigma}_{t}^{2}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{-}

\displaystyle\frac{3(\alpha_{t+1}-\overline{\alpha}_{t+1})\widetilde{\sigma}_{t+1}^{2}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t+1}}{=}\frac{\widetilde{\sigma}_{t}^{2}\eta_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{+}3\left(1-\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\right)\widetilde{\sigma}_{t}^{2}{-}3\left(1-\frac{1-\alpha_{t+1}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t+1}}\right)\widetilde{\sigma}_{t+1}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{3(1-\alpha_{t})\widetilde{\sigma}_{t}^{2}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}-\frac{3(1-\alpha_{t})\widetilde{\sigma}_{t}^{2}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}+\frac{3(1-\alpha_{t+1})\widetilde{\sigma}_{t+1}^{2}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t+1}}+3(\widetilde{\sigma}_{t}^{2}-\widetilde{\sigma}_{t+1}^{2})

\displaystyle\leq 3\left(\frac{1-\alpha_{t+1}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t+1}}\right)^{2}\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{t+1}^{2}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t+1}}+\frac{C_{6}\log^{2}T}{T^{2}}\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\leq\frac{2C_{6}\log^{2}T}{T^{2}}\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}.

As a consequence, we can demonstrate that

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{{t}={1}}^{T}{\mathcal{S}}_{t,1}

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\leq}\left(\frac{2C_{6}\log T}{T}\right)^{2}\sum\limits_{{t}={1}}^{T-1}\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathrm{Cov}_{0|t}(X_{t})\right)\right]+\frac{2C_{6}\log T}{T}\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{T}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{T}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathrm{Cov}_{0|T}(X_{T})\right)\right]+\frac{1}{T^{9}}

(83)

\displaystyle\overset{\text{(a)}}{\leq}C_{3}kT\log T\left(\frac{2C_{6}\log T}{T}\right)^{2}+\frac{2C_{3}C_{6}k\log^{2}T}{T}+\frac{1}{T^{9}}\leq C_{9}\frac{k\log^{3}T}{T}+\frac{1}{T^{9}}\leq C_{10}\frac{k\log^{3}T}{T},

where (a) applies the moment inequality (
44
) with
l=2
.

Next, we turn to
\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\mathcal{S}}_{t,2}
, which concerns the cumulative estimation error. Given that

\frac{\eta_{t}}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\leq\frac{\eta_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\sqrt{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\leq\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\sqrt{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\leq\frac{8c_{1}\log T}{T}\sqrt{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}
, we can derive

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{{t}={2}}^{T}{\mathcal{S}}_{t,2}

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{{t}={2}}^{T}\frac{2\eta_{t}\sqrt{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\bigg\{\frac{\varepsilon_{\mathsf{score},t}}{\sqrt{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Jacobi},1,t}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Jacobi},2,t}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Hess},t}\bigg\}

(84)

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{{t}={2}}^{T}\frac{68(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})\eta_{t}^{2}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Jacobi},1,t}^{2}+\sum\limits_{{t}={2}}^{T}\frac{\eta_{t}^{2}}{2(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\varepsilon_{\mathsf{score},t}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8c_{1}\log T}{T}\bigg\{\sum\limits_{{t}={1}}^{T}\sqrt{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\varepsilon_{\mathsf{score},t}{+}\sum\limits_{{t}={2}}^{T}(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Jacobi},1,t}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{{t}={2}}^{T}(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Jacobi},2,t}{+}\sum\limits_{{t}={2}}^{T}(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Hess},t}\bigg\}

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{10}\log^{2}T}{T^{2}}\left\{\sum\limits_{{t}={1}}^{T}(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Jacobi},1,t}^{2}+\sum\limits_{{t}={1}}^{T}(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}\varepsilon_{\mathsf{score},t}^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{(a)}}{\leq}8c_{1}\left(\varepsilon_{\mathsf{score}}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Jacobi},1}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Jacobi},2}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Hess}}\right)\log T+\frac{C_{10}\log^{2}T}{T}\left(\varepsilon_{\mathsf{score}}^{2}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Jacobi},1}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C_{11}\left(\varepsilon_{\mathsf{score}}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Jacobi},1}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Jacobi},2}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Hess}}\right)\log T,

where (a) results from the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and Assumption
3
, and the last inequality holds provided that
\frac{\log T}{T}(\varepsilon_{\mathsf{score}}+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Jacobi},1})\leq 1
.

Appendix D
Analysis for DDPM (proof of Theorem
5
)

Given that Theorem
4
is a special case of Theorem
5
,
we shall focus on proving Theorem
5
in this section.

D.1
Preparation

Before proceeding, let us introduce several convention and auxiliary objects that will be useful throughout.

Random vectors in the extended space.

Firstly,
the random vectors in this proof are allowed to take values in the extended space
{\mathbb{R}}^{d}\cup\{\infty\}
that covers the point
\infty
(think about it as infinity in
d
dimension). Namely, they can be constructed in the following way:

X=\begin{cases}X^{\prime},&\text{with probability }\theta,\\
\infty,&\text{with probability }1-\theta,\end{cases}

where
\theta\in[0,1]
and
X^{\prime}

is a random vector in
{\mathbb{R}}^{d}
in the usual sense. If
X^{\prime}
has a density
p_{X^{\prime}}
,
then the generalized density of
X
is

p_{X}(x)=\theta p_{X^{\prime}}(x)\mathbbm{1}\{x\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d}\}+(1-\theta)\delta_{\infty},

where
\delta_{\infty}
indicates the Dirac measure at
\infty
.

Introducing auxiliary sequences.

Secondly, let us introduce several auxiliary sequences that shall play a pivotal role in our analysis. Here and throughout, the notation
X\,|\,Y\sim\widetilde{X}\,|\,\widetilde{Y}
means that the conditional density of
X
given
Y=y
and that of
\widetilde{X}
given
\widetilde{Y}=y
coincide for any
y
.

•

First, we define a discrete-time reverse process
\{Y_{t}^{\star}\}_{t=T}^{1}
by

\displaystyle Y_{T}^{\star}=Y_{T}\sim{\mathcal{N}}(0,I_{d}),\qquad Y_{t-1}^{\star}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha}_{t}}\big(Y_{t}^{\star}+\eta_{t}s_{t}^{\star}(Y_{t}^{\star})+\sigma_{t}W_{t}\big).

(85)

In short, this auxiliary process
\{Y^{\star}\}
implements DDPM using exact score functions.

•

Based on the above process, we construct an auxiliary reverse process
\overline{Y}_{t}
that follows the same transition dynamics as
Y_{t}^{\star}
in the absence of score estimation errors:

\displaystyle\overline{Y}_{t-1}^{-}\,|\,\overline{Y}_{t}\sim Y_{t-1}^{\star}\,|\,Y_{t}^{\star},\qquad\overline{Y}_{t}\,|\,\{\overline{Y}_{t}^{-}=y_{t}^{-}\}=\begin{cases}y_{t}^{-},&\text{with prob. }\frac{p_{X_{t}}(y_{t}^{-})}{p_{\overline{Y}_{t}^{-}}(y_{t}^{-})}\wedge 1\\
\infty,&\text{otherwise}\end{cases}

(86)

for any
y_{t}^{-}\neq\infty
,
where we recall that
a\wedge b\coloneqq\min\{a,b\}
.
It is straightforward to show that

p_{\overline{Y}_{t}}(y_{t})=\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{d}}\left(p_{X_{t}}(y_{t}^{-})\wedge p_{\overline{Y}_{t}^{-}}(y_{t}^{-})\right)\delta(y_{t}-y_{t}^{-}){\textnormal{d}}y_{t}^{-}=p_{X_{t}}(y_{t})\wedge p_{\overline{Y}_{t}^{-}}(y_{t})

(87)

for any
y_{t}\neq\infty
,
where
\delta(\cdot)
denotes the Dirac measure.

•

To account for the score estimation error, we introduce another auxiliary reverse process
\widehat{Y}_{t}
based on the dynamics of
Y_{t}
:

\displaystyle\widehat{Y}_{t-1}^{-}\,|\,\widehat{Y}_{t}\sim Y_{t-1}\,|\,Y_{t},\qquad\widehat{Y}_{t}\,|\,\{\widehat{Y}_{t}^{-}=y_{t}^{-}\}=\begin{cases}y_{t}^{-},&\text{with prob. }\frac{p_{X_{t}}(y_{t}^{-})}{p_{\overline{Y}_{t}^{-}}(y_{t}^{-})}\wedge 1,\\
\infty,&\text{otherwise}.\end{cases}

(88)

It is seen that the probability densities of
Y_{t}
and
\widehat{Y}_{t}
satisfy the properties stated in the following lemma, whose proof can be found in Appendix
D.3
.

Lemma 13

For all
t=1,\cdots,T
, it holds that

p_{Y_{t}}(x)\geq p_{\widehat{Y}_{t}}(x),\qquad\text{for all }x\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d}.

(89)

D.2
Main steps for proving Theorem
5

We are now in a position to present the main steps of our proof.

Step 1: linking the TV distances between adjacent steps.

Define the following set

\displaystyle{\mathcal{A}}_{t}\coloneqq\Big\{x:p_{X_{t}}(x)\geq p_{\overline{Y}_{t}^{-}}(x)\Big\}.

(90)

In view of (
87
), the condition
x\in\mathcal{A}_{t}
is equivalent to
p_{\overline{Y}_{t}}(x)\leq p_{X_{t}}(x)
, which together with some well-known property of the TV distance yields

\displaystyle\mathsf{TV}(p_{X_{t}},p_{\overline{Y}_{t}})=\int_{x:p_{\overline{Y}_{t}}(x)\leq p_{X_{t}}(x)}\big(p_{X_{t}}(x)-p_{\overline{Y}_{t}}(x)\big){\textnormal{d}}x=\int_{{\mathcal{A}_{t}}}\big(p_{X_{t}}(x)-p_{\overline{Y}_{t}}(x)\big){\textnormal{d}}x.

(91)

To link the iterates in step
t
and step
t-1
, we observe that the density
p_{\overline{Y}_{t-1}^{-}}(\cdot)
satisfies

\displaystyle p_{\overline{Y}_{t-1}^{-}}(x_{t-1})=\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{d}}p_{\overline{Y}_{t-1}^{-}\,|\,\overline{Y}_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})p_{\overline{Y}_{t}}(x_{t}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}=\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{d}}p_{{Y}_{t-1}^{\star}|Y_{t}^{\star}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})p_{\overline{Y}_{t}}(x_{t}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}

\displaystyle\quad=\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{d}}p_{Y_{t-1}^{\star}\,|\,Y_{t}^{\star}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})p_{X_{t}}(x_{t}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}+\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{d}}p_{Y_{t-1}^{\star}\,|\,Y_{t}^{\star}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})\big(p_{\overline{Y}_{t}}(x_{t})-p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})\big){\textnormal{d}}x_{t},

(92)

where we have utilized the construction in (
86
).
With the preceding two identities
in place, we can further derive the following recursion for all
t\geq 2
:

\displaystyle\mathsf{TV}(p_{X_{t-1}},p_{\overline{Y}_{t-1}})

\displaystyle=\int_{{\mathcal{A}}_{t-1}}\big(p_{X_{t-1}}(x_{t-1}){-}p_{\overline{Y}_{t-1}}(x_{t-1})\big){\textnormal{d}}x_{t-1}

(93)

\displaystyle\overset{\text{(a)}}{=}\int_{{\mathcal{A}}_{t-1}}\big(p_{X_{t-1}}(x_{t-1}){-}p_{\overline{Y}_{t-1}^{-}}(x_{t-1})\big){\textnormal{d}}x_{t-1}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\int_{{\mathcal{A}}_{t-1}}p_{X_{t-1}}(x_{t-1})-\int_{{\mathcal{A}}_{t-1}\times{\mathbb{R}}^{d}}p_{Y_{t-1}^{\star}\,|\,Y_{t}^{\star}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})p_{X_{t}}(x_{t}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t-1}{\textnormal{d}}x_{t}}_{\eqqcolon\,{\mathcal{R}}_{t-1}}

\displaystyle+\int_{{\mathcal{A}}_{t-1}\times{\mathbb{R}}^{d}}p_{Y_{t-1}^{\star}\,|\,Y_{t}^{\star}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})\big(p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})-p_{\overline{Y}_{t}}(x_{t})\big){\textnormal{d}}x_{t-1}{\textnormal{d}}x_{t}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{(b)}}{\leq}{\mathcal{R}}_{t-1}+\int_{{\mathcal{A}}_{t-1}\times{\mathcal{A}}_{t}}p_{Y_{t-1}^{\star}\,|\,Y_{t}^{\star}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})\big(p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})-p_{\overline{Y}_{t}}(x_{t})\big){\textnormal{d}}x_{t-1}{\textnormal{d}}x_{t}

\displaystyle={\mathcal{R}}_{t-1}+\int_{{\mathcal{A}}_{t}}{\mathbb{P}}_{Y_{t-1}^{\star}\,|\,Y_{t}^{\star}}({\mathcal{A}}_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})\big(p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})-p_{\overline{Y}_{t}}(x_{t})\big){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{(c)}}{\leq}{\mathcal{R}}_{t-1}+\mathsf{TV}(p_{X_{t}},p_{\overline{Y}_{t}}).

Here, (a) follows since
p_{\overline{Y}_{t-1}}=p_{\overline{Y}_{t-1}^{-}}
on
{\mathcal{A}}_{t-1}
(see (
87
)), (b) holds since
p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})-p_{\overline{Y}_{t}}(x_{t})\leq 0
on
{\mathcal{A}}_{t}^{\mathrm{c}}
, while (c) is valid due to (
91
) and the fact that
{\mathbb{P}}_{Y_{t-1}^{\star}\,|\,Y_{t}^{\star}}({\mathcal{A}}_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})\leq 1
for all
x_{t}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d}
.
Importantly, the recursion (
93
) indicates that each iteration of DDPM can increase the TV distance of interest by at most
\mathcal{R}_{t-1}
.

It then boils down to controlling
\mathcal{R}_{t-1}
. Towards this end,
we would like to decompose

\displaystyle{\mathcal{R}}_{t-1}=\int_{{\mathcal{A}}_{t-1}}{\mathcal{R}}_{t-1}(x_{t-1}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t-1},

(94)

where we define

\displaystyle{\mathcal{R}}_{t-1}(x_{t-1})\coloneqq p_{X_{t-1}}(x_{t-1})-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}p_{Y_{t-1}^{\star}\,|\,Y_{t}^{\star}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})p_{X_{t}}(x_{t}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t}.

(95)

In the ensuing steps,
we shall bound
{\mathcal{R}}_{t-1}(x_{t-1})
for
x_{t-1}\in{\mathcal{A}}_{t-1}
, which in turn facilitates analysis for
{\mathcal{R}}_{t-1}
.

Step 2: decomposing and calculating
{\mathcal{R}}_{t-1}(x_{t-1})
.

In this step, we intend to calculate the function
{\mathcal{R}}_{t-1}(x_{t-1})
defined in (
95
).
For notational convenience, for any vector
x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
we shall denote

\displaystyle u_{t}(x)\coloneqq x+\eta_{t}s_{t}^{\star}(x)

(96)

in the sequel.
Further, we present the following useful lemma, whose proof can be found in Appendix
D.4
.

Lemma 14

For any
t=1,\cdots,T
, the mapping
u_{t}:{\mathbb{R}}^{d}\to{\mathbb{R}}^{d}
is a
C^{1}-
diffeomorphism.

For convenience in the following discussion, we denote the
inverse
of
u_{t}
by
x_{t}(\cdot)
.

Equipped with the quantities
\{\overline{\alpha}_{t}\}
(cf. (
8
)) and the update rule (
85
) of
\{Y_{t}^{\star}\}
, we can demonstrate that, for each
t\geq 2
,

\displaystyle p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{0})

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{2\pi(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\right)^{\frac{d}{2}}\exp\left(-\frac{\left\|{x_{t-1}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}x_{0}}\right\|_{2}^{2}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1})}\right),

\displaystyle p_{Y_{t-1}^{\star}\,|\,Y_{t}^{\star}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{2\pi\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)^{\frac{d}{2}}\exp\left(-\frac{\left\|{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}x_{t-1}-u_{t}}\right\|_{2}^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right).

Clearly, both
p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{0}}
and
p_{Y_{t-1}^{\star}\,|\,Y_{t}^{\star}}
are density functions of conditional Gaussians. In light of this, it turns out that we can find another conditional Gaussian distribution
\widetilde{p}_{U_{t}|X_{0}}(u_{t}\,|\,x_{0})
satisfying the following convolution formula:

p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{0})=\int_{x_{t}}p_{Y_{t-1}^{\star}\,|\,Y_{t}^{\star}}\big(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t}(u_{t})\big)\widetilde{p}_{U_{t}|X_{0}}(u_{t}\,|\,x_{0}){\textnormal{d}}u_{t},\quad\forall x_{t-1}\in{\mathcal{A}}_{t-1},~x_{0}\in{\mathcal{X}}_{\mathsf{data}},

(97)

with the exact form of
\widetilde{p}_{U_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}
provided in the following lemma. The proof is deferred to Appendix
D.5
.

Lemma 15

The probability density function
\widetilde{p}_{U_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(u_{t}\,|\,x_{0})
is given by

\widetilde{p}_{U_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(u_{t}\,|\,x_{0})=\left(\frac{1}{2\pi(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}-\sigma_{t}^{2})}\right)^{d/2}\exp\left\{-\frac{\left\|{u_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}}\right\|_{2}^{2}}{2(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}-\sigma_{t}^{2})}\right\}.

Armed with the above density function and (
97
), we can deduce the following lemma whose proof is deferred to Appendix
D.6
.

Lemma 16

For any
t=2,\cdots,T
and any
x_{t-1}\in{\mathcal{A}}_{t-1}
,
{\mathcal{R}}_{t-1}(x_{t-1})
can be expressed as

{\mathcal{R}}_{t-1}(x_{t-1})=\int p_{Y_{t-1}^{\star}\mid Y_{t}^{\star}}(x_{t-1}\mid x_{t})p_{X_{t}\mid X_{0}}(x_{t}\mid x_{0})\big({\mathcal{G}}(x_{t},x_{0})-1\big)p_{X_{0}}(x_{0}){\textnormal{d}}x_{0}{\textnormal{d}}x_{t}.

with the definition of
{\mathcal{G}}(x_{t},x_{0})
given by

{\mathcal{G}}(x_{t},x_{0})\coloneqq\frac{\widetilde{p}_{U_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(u_{t}(x_{t})\,|\,x_{0})}{p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0})}\det\left(\frac{{\textnormal{d}}u_{t}}{{\textnormal{d}}x_{t}}\right)

Given that both
\widetilde{p}_{U_{t}|X_{0}}
and
p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}
represent Gaussian distributions, we can obtain that

\displaystyle{\mathcal{G}}(x_{t},x_{0})

\displaystyle=\det\left(\frac{{\textnormal{d}}\big(x_{t}+\eta_{t}s_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\big)}{{\textnormal{d}}x_{t}}\right)\cdot\frac{\left(\overline{\sigma}_{t}^{2}\right)^{-d/2}\exp\Big\{-\frac{\|u_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\|_{2}^{2}}{2\overline{\sigma}_{t}^{2}}\Big\}}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{-d/2}\exp\Big\{-\frac{\|x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\|_{2}^{2}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\Big\}}

(98)

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\det\left(\frac{{\textnormal{d}}\big(x_{t}+\eta_{t}s_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\big)}{{\textnormal{d}}x_{t}}\right)\cdot\frac{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{d/2}}{\overline{\sigma}_{t}^{d}}}_{\eqqcolon\,{\mathcal{G}}_{1}(x_{t},x_{0})}\cdot\underbrace{\exp\left\{\frac{\left\|{x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}}\right\|_{2}^{2}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}-\frac{\left\|{u_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}}\right\|_{2}^{2}}{2\overline{\sigma}_{t}^{2}}\right\}}_{\eqqcolon\,{\mathcal{G}}_{2}(x_{t},x_{0})}.

Taken Lemma
16
and (
98
) collectively, the above results demonstrate that

{\mathcal{R}}_{t-1}(x_{t-1})=\int p_{Y_{t-1}^{\star}\,|\,Y_{t}^{\star}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(u_{t}\,|\,x_{0})\big({\mathcal{G}}_{1}(x_{t},x_{0}){\mathcal{G}}_{2}(x_{t},x_{0})-1\big)p_{X_{0}}(x_{0}){\textnormal{d}}x_{0}{\textnormal{d}}x_{t}.

(99)

Step 3: determining the exponent of the product
{\mathcal{G}}_{1}(x_{t},x_{0}){\mathcal{G}}_{2}(x_{t},x_{0})
.

In order to control
(
99
) further, one needs to cope with the product
{\mathcal{G}}_{1}(x_{t},x_{0}){\mathcal{G}}_{2}(x_{t},x_{0})
. Towards this end, we find it helpful to introduce the following functions:

\displaystyle\zeta_{t}(x_{t},x_{0})

\displaystyle\coloneqq\left(\frac{\eta_{t}}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})\overline{\sigma}_{t}^{2}}-\frac{\eta_{t}^{2}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}\overline{\sigma}_{t}^{2}}\right)\left\{\left\|{\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0\,|\,t}(x_{t})-x_{t}}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\left\|{\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}-x_{t}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\eta_{t}}{\overline{\sigma}_{t}^{2}(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}}\big(x_{0}-\mu_{0\,|\,t}(x_{t})\big)^{\top}\big(\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\mu_{0\,|\,t}(x_{t})-x_{t}\big)

\displaystyle\qquad-\left(\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{t}\eta_{t}}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})\overline{\sigma}_{t}^{2}}-\frac{\eta_{t}^{2}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}\overline{\sigma}_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{tr}\big(\mathrm{Cov}_{0\,|\,t}(x_{t})\big),

(100a)

\displaystyle Z_{t}(x_{t})

\displaystyle\coloneqq\log\det\left(I+\frac{\eta_{t}}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}-\eta_{t})}\mathrm{Cov}_{0\,|\,t}(x_{t})\right)

\displaystyle\qquad-\left(\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{t}\eta_{t}}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})\overline{\sigma}_{t}^{2}}-\frac{\eta_{t}^{2}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}\overline{\sigma}_{t}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{tr}\big(\mathrm{Cov}_{0\,|\,t}(x_{t})\big),

(100b)

where we recall the definition of
\mu_{0\,|\,t}
and
\mathrm{Cov}_{0\,|\,t}
in (
42
). The following lemma asserts that these two functions can be harnessed to represent
{\mathcal{G}}_{1}(x_{t},x_{0}){\mathcal{G}}_{2}(x_{t},x_{0})
.

Lemma 17

The quantities
{\mathcal{G}}_{i}(x_{t},x_{0}),~i=1,2
defined in (
98
) satisfy

{\mathcal{G}}_{1}(x_{t},x_{0}){\mathcal{G}}_{2}(x_{t},x_{0})=e^{\zeta_{t}(x_{t},x_{0})}\cdot e^{Z_{t}(x_{t})}.

Further, it holds that

\int\zeta_{t}(x_{t},x_{0})p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x_{t}){\textnormal{d}}x_{0}=0\qquad\text{ for all }x_{t}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d}.

In short, Lemma
17
determines the exponent of
{\mathcal{G}}_{1}(x_{t},x_{0}){\mathcal{G}}_{2}(x_{t},x_{0})
, where one of the two components satisfies
\mathbb{E}[\zeta_{t}(X_{t},X_{0})\,|\,X_{t}=x_{t}]=0
for an arbitrary
x_{t}
.
The proof of this lemma is postponed to Appendix
D.7
.

Step 4: bounding
{\mathcal{R}}_{t-1}
using
Z_{t}(X_{t})
.

With the above calculations of
{\mathcal{R}}_{t-1}(x_{t-1})
in place, we are now ready to bound
{\mathcal{R}}_{t-1}
.
In view of Lemma
17
, for any
x_{t}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
one has

\int\left(e^{\zeta_{t}(x_{t},x_{0})}-1\right)e^{Z_{t}(x_{t})}p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x_{t}){\textnormal{d}}x_{0}\geq e^{Z_{t}(x_{t})}\int\zeta_{t}(x_{t},x_{0})p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x_{t}){\textnormal{d}}x_{0}=0,

(101)

where we have invoked the elementary inequality
e^{x}-1\geq x
.
With (
99
) and (
101
) in place, we can show that

\displaystyle{\mathcal{R}}_{t-1}

\displaystyle=\int_{{\mathcal{A}}_{t-1}}{\mathcal{R}}_{t-1}(x_{t-1}){\textnormal{d}}x_{t-1}{=}\int_{{\mathcal{A}}_{t-1}\times{\mathbb{R}}^{d}\times{\mathcal{X}}_{\mathsf{data}}}p_{Y_{t-1}^{\star}\,|\,Y_{t}^{\star}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})p_{X_{t},X_{0}}(x_{t},x_{0})\big({\mathcal{G}}(x_{t},x_{0})-1\big){\textnormal{d}}x_{0}{\textnormal{d}}x_{t}{\textnormal{d}}x_{t-1}

(102)

\displaystyle=\int_{{\mathcal{A}}_{t-1}\times{\mathbb{R}}^{d}\times{\mathcal{X}}_{\mathsf{data}}}p_{Y_{t-1}^{\star}\,|\,Y_{t}^{\star}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})p_{X_{t},X_{0}}(x_{t},x_{0})\big\{{\mathcal{G}}_{1}(x_{t},x_{0}){\mathcal{G}}_{2}(x_{t},x_{0})-1\big\}{\textnormal{d}}x_{0}{\textnormal{d}}x_{t}{\textnormal{d}}x_{t-1}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{(a)}}{=}\int_{{\mathcal{A}}_{t-1}\times{\mathbb{R}}^{d}\times{\mathcal{X}}_{\mathsf{data}}}p_{Y_{t-1}^{\star}\,|\,Y_{t}^{\star}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})p_{X_{t},X_{0}}(x_{t},x_{0})\left\{e^{\zeta_{t}(x_{t},x_{0})+Z_{t}(x_{t})}-1\right\}{\textnormal{d}}x_{0}{\textnormal{d}}x_{t}{\textnormal{d}}x_{t-1}

\displaystyle\leq\int{\mathbb{P}}_{Y_{t-1}^{\star}\,|\,Y_{t}^{\star}}\left({\mathcal{A}}_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t}\right)p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})\left\{\int(e^{\zeta_{t}(x_{t},x_{0})}{-}1)e^{Z_{t}(x_{t})}p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x_{t}){\textnormal{d}}x_{0}{+}\big|e^{Z_{t}(x_{t})}{-}1\big|\right\}{\textnormal{d}}x_{t}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{(b)}}{\leq}\int p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})\left\{\int(e^{\zeta_{t}(x_{t},x_{0})}{-}1)e^{Z_{t}(x_{t})}p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x_{t}){\textnormal{d}}x_{0}{+}\big|e^{Z_{t}(x_{t})}{-}1\big|\right\}{\textnormal{d}}x_{t}

\displaystyle=\int p_{X_{t},X_{0}}(x_{t},x_{0})e^{\zeta_{t}(x_{t},x_{0})+Z_{t}(x_{t})}{\textnormal{d}}x_{t}{\textnormal{d}}x_{0}-\int p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})e^{Z_{t}(x_{t})}{\textnormal{d}}x_{t}+\int p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})\big|e^{Z_{t}(x_{t})}-1\big|{\textnormal{d}}x_{t}.

Here
(a)
follows from Lemma
17
, and
(b)
holds according to
{\mathbb{P}}_{Y_{t-1}^{\star}\mid Y_{t}^{\star}}({\mathcal{A}}_{t-1}\mid x_{t})
.
Note that
e^{\zeta_{t}(x_{t},x_{0})+Z_{t}(x_{t})}={\mathcal{G}}(x_{t},x_{0})=\frac{\widetilde{p}_{U_{t}|X_{0}}(u_{t}\,|\,x_{0})}{p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0})}\det\big(\frac{{\textnormal{d}}u_{t}}{{\textnormal{d}}x_{t}}\big)
, and as a result,

\int p_{X_{t},X_{0}}(x_{t},x_{0})e^{\zeta_{t}(x_{t},x_{0})+Z_{t}(x_{t})}{\textnormal{d}}x_{t}{\textnormal{d}}x_{0}=\int\widetilde{p}_{U_{t}|X_{0}}(u_{t}\,|\,x_{0})p_{X_{0}}(x_{0}){\textnormal{d}}u_{t}{\textnormal{d}}x_{0}=1.

Substitution into (
102
) yields

\displaystyle{\mathcal{R}}_{t-1}

\displaystyle\leq 1-\int p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})e^{Z_{t}(x_{t})}{\textnormal{d}}x_{t}+\int p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})\big|e^{Z_{t}(x_{t})}-1\big|{\textnormal{d}}x_{t}

(103)

\displaystyle=2\int p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})\big(1-e^{Z_{t}(x_{t})}\big)_{+}{\textnormal{d}}x_{t}\leq 2{\mathbb{E}}\big[\left(-Z_{t}(X_{t})\right)_{+}\big],

where the equality in the last line arises from the elementary inequality
1-z+|1-z|=2(1-z)_{+}
, and the last inequality holds by combining
1-e^{x}\leq-x
and the non-decreasing property of the function
(\cdot)_{+}
.

Step 5: establishing recursions for
\mathsf{TV}(p_{X_{t}},p_{\overline{Y}_{t}})
with the aid of conditional covariances.

From Eqn. (
103
), we know that
{\mathcal{R}}_{t-1}
is upper bounded by
{\mathbb{E}}\big[\big(-Z_{t}(X_{t})\big)_{+}\big]
, an object that can be further controlled through
the following lemma. The proof can be found in Appendix
D.8
.

Lemma 18

For any iteration
t
, one has

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\big[\big(-Z_{t}(X_{t})\big)_{+}\big]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8\overline{\alpha}_{t}\eta_{t}^{2}}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{3}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathrm{tr}\big(\mathrm{Cov}_{0\,|\,t}(X_{t})\big)\right]

(104)

\displaystyle+C\widetilde{\sigma}_{t}^{2}\Big\{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathrm{tr}\big(\mathrm{Cov}_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(X_{t})\big)\right]-{\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathrm{tr}\big(\mathrm{Cov}_{X_{0}|X_{t-1}}(X_{t-1})\big)\right]\Big\}+\frac{C}{T^{10}},

where
C>0
is some universal constant.

Taking (
93
), (
103
) and (
104
) collectively yields

\displaystyle\mathsf{TV}(p_{X_{t-1}},p_{\overline{Y}_{t-1}})

\displaystyle\leq{\mathcal{R}}_{t-1}+\mathsf{TV}(p_{X_{t}},p_{\overline{Y}_{t}})\leq\mathsf{TV}(p_{X_{t}},p_{\overline{Y}_{t}})+{\mathbb{E}}\big[(-Z_{t}(X_{t}))_{+}\big]

(105)

\displaystyle\leq\mathsf{TV}(p_{X_{t}},p_{\overline{Y}_{t}}){+}\left(C\widetilde{\sigma}_{t}^{2}{+}\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{t}\eta_{t}^{2}}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{3}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathrm{tr}\big(\mathrm{Cov}_{0\mid t}(X_{t})\big)\right]

\displaystyle-{C\widetilde{\sigma}_{t}^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathrm{tr}\left(\mathrm{Cov}_{0|t-1}(X_{t-1})\right)\right]{+}\frac{C}{T^{10}}.

Step 6: controlling the effect of score estimation errors.

Recall that the process
\{\overline{Y}_{t}\}
is constructed without incorporating score matching errors, and hence we still need to quantify the influence of inexact scores upon convergence.
Towards this, apply the data processing inequality w.r.t. KL divergence to obtain

\displaystyle\mathsf{KL}(p_{\overline{Y}_{1}}\parallel p_{\widehat{Y}_{1}})\leq\mathsf{KL}\left(p_{\overline{Y}_{1},\overline{Y}_{1}^{-},\cdots,\overline{Y}_{T},\overline{Y}_{T}^{-}}~\big\|~p_{\widehat{Y}_{1},\widehat{Y}_{1}^{-},\cdots,\widehat{Y}_{T},\widehat{Y}_{T}^{-}}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{\text{(a)}}{=}\mathsf{KL}(p_{\overline{Y}_{T}^{-}}\parallel p_{\widehat{Y}_{T}^{-}})+\sum\limits_{{t}={2}}^{T}{\mathbb{E}}_{x_{t}\sim p_{\overline{Y}_{t}}}\left[\mathsf{KL}\left(p_{\overline{Y}_{t-1}^{-}\,|\,\overline{Y}_{t}=x_{t}}~\big\|~p_{\widehat{Y}_{t-1}^{-}|\widehat{Y}_{t}=x_{t}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum\limits_{{t}={2}}^{T}{\mathbb{E}}_{x_{t}\sim p_{\overline{Y}_{t}^{-}}}\left[\mathsf{KL}\left(p_{\overline{Y}_{t-1}|\overline{Y}_{t}^{-}=x_{t}}~\big\|~p_{\widehat{Y}_{t-1}|\widehat{Y}_{t}^{-}=x_{t}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\text{(b)}}{=}\sum\limits_{{t}={2}}^{T}{\mathbb{E}}_{x_{t}\sim p_{\overline{Y}_{t}}}\left[\mathsf{KL}\left(p_{\overline{Y}_{t-1}^{-}\,|\,\overline{Y}_{t}=x_{t}}~\big\|~p_{\widehat{Y}_{t-1}^{-}|\widehat{Y}_{t}=x_{t}}\right)\right]\overset{\text{(c)}}{=}\sum\limits_{{t}={2}}^{T}{\mathbb{E}}_{x_{t}\sim p_{\overline{Y}_{t}}}\left[\mathsf{KL}\left(p_{{Y}_{t-1}^{\star}|Y_{t}^{\star}=x_{t}}~\big\|~p_{{Y}_{t-1}|{Y}_{t}=x_{t}}\right)\right].

Here, (a) follows from the chain rule of KL divergence, (b) holds since the conditional distribution of
\widehat{Y}_{t}
given
\widehat{Y}_{t}^{-}=x
and that of
\overline{Y}_{t}
given
\overline{Y}_{t}^{-}=x
are identical, while (c) arises from the construction of
\overline{Y}_{t-1}^{-}\,|\,\overline{Y}_{t}
and
\widehat{Y}_{t-1}^{-}\,|\,\widehat{Y}_{t}
(see (
86
) and (
88
)).

Recall that
Y_{t-1}^{\star}\,|\,Y_{t}^{\star}=x_{t}
(see (
85
)) and
Y_{t-1}\,|\,Y_{t}=x_{t}
are two Gaussian distributions given by

Y_{t-1}^{\star}\,|\,Y_{t}^{\star}=x_{t}~\sim{\mathcal{N}}\left(\frac{x_{t}+\eta_{t}s_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}},\sigma_{t}^{2}I_{d}\right),\quad Y_{t-1}\,|\,Y_{t}=x_{t}~\sim{\mathcal{N}}\left(\frac{x_{t}+\eta_{t}s_{t}(x_{t})}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}},\sigma_{t}^{2}I_{d}\right)

with
\eta_{t},~\sigma_{t}^{2}
satisfying
\eta_{t}^{2}\leq C(1-\alpha_{t})\sigma_{t}^{2}
. Further, the KL divergence between two Gaussian measures admits a closed-form expression, i.e.,

\displaystyle\mathsf{KL}\left(p_{Y_{t-1}^{\star}\,|\,Y_{t}^{\star}}(\cdot|x_{t})\parallel p_{Y_{t-1}|Y_{t}}(\cdot|x_{t})\right)=\mathsf{KL}\left({\mathcal{N}}\left(\frac{x_{t}+\eta_{t}s_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}},\sigma_{t}^{2}I_{d}\right)~\bigg\|~{\mathcal{N}}\left(\frac{x_{t}+\eta_{t}s_{t}(x_{t})}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}},\sigma_{t}^{2}I_{d}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta_{t}^{2}/\alpha_{t}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\left\|{s_{t}(x_{t})-s_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})}\right\|_{2}^{2}=\frac{\eta_{t}^{2}/\alpha_{t}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\left\|{\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})}\right\|_{2}^{2}=\frac{C(1-\alpha_{t})}{2\alpha_{t}}\left\|{\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})}\right\|_{2}^{2}\leq C(1-\alpha_{t})\left\|{\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})}\right\|_{2}^{2}.

Therefore, we can control the KL divergence between
\overline{Y}_{1}
(the auxiliary process without score errors) and
\widehat{Y}_{1}
(the auxiliary process with score errors) as follows:

\displaystyle\mathsf{KL}(p_{\overline{Y}_{1}}\parallel p_{\widehat{Y}_{1}})

\displaystyle\leq\sum\limits_{{t}={2}}^{T}{\mathbb{E}}_{x_{t}\sim p_{\overline{Y}_{t}}}\left[\mathsf{KL}\left(p_{{Y}_{t-1}^{\star}\,|\,Y_{t}^{\star}=x_{t}}~\big\|~p_{{Y}_{t-1}\,|\,{Y}_{t}=x_{t}}\right)\right]

(106)

\displaystyle\leq\sum\limits_{{t}={2}}^{T}C(1-\alpha_{t}){\mathbb{E}}_{x_{t}\sim p_{\overline{Y}_{t}}}\left[\left\|{\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]\overset{\text{(a)}}{\leq}\sum\limits_{{t}={2}}^{T}C(1-\alpha_{t}){\mathbb{E}}_{x_{t}\sim p_{X_{t}}}\left[\left\|{\varepsilon^{\mathsf{sc}}_{t}(x_{t})}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{c_{1}C\log T}{T}\sum\limits_{{t}={2}}^{T}\varepsilon_{\mathsf{score},t}^{2}\leq c_{1}C(\log T)\varepsilon_{\mathsf{score}}^{2},

where (a) follows from (
87
).

Step 7: putting all pieces together.

Applying inequality (
105
) from
1
to
T
recursively, we arrive at

\displaystyle\mathsf{TV}(p_{X_{1}},p_{\overline{Y}_{1}})
